@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.BalancedHulls.observations : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {Γ : Type} [inst : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ]
  (v : Valuation SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType Γ),
  (∀ (x y : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ v x ≤ v y) →
    ∀ (δ : SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma),
      1 < δ →
        ∀ {R : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF × SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF)}
          {f : SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF → SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF}
          {t₁ t₂ : SpecialTransformations.Section5.RV},
          IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.IsBalanced R f t₁ t₂ →
            t₁ ≠ SpecialTransformations.Section5.rv 0 →
              t₂ ≠ SpecialTransformations.Section5.rv 0 →
                ∀ a ∈ IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.first R,
                  ∃ x ∈ IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.first R,
                    ∃ y ∈ IntegrationInACVF.Section4.BalancedFibres.fibre R a,
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vfValueHom (x - a) =
                          SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv t₁ ∧
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vfValueHom (y - f a) =
                            SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv t₂ ∧
                          IntegrationInACVF.Section3.FiniteClosedHull.IsHull v
                              (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.first R ∩
                                f ⁻¹' IntegrationInACVF.Section4.BalancedFibres.fibre R a)
                              a (x - a) ∧
                            IntegrationInACVF.Section3.FiniteClosedHull.IsHull v
                                (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.BalancedFibres.fibre R a) (f a) (y - f a) ∧
                              a ∉
                                  IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.first R ∩
                                    f ⁻¹' IntegrationInACVF.Section4.BalancedFibres.fibre R a ∧
                                f a ∉ IntegrationInACVF.Section4.BalancedFibres.fibre R a ∧
                                  ∀ (r₁ r₂ : IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.PaperBalls.Radius),
                                    IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.PaperBalls.HasRadius
                                        (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.rawSet
                                          (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.first R))
                                        r₁ →
                                      IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.PaperBalls.HasRadius
                                          (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.rawSet
                                            (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.second R))
                                          r₂ →
                                        r₁ < ↑(SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv t₁) ∧
                                          r₂ < ↑(SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv t₂)

            ↑t = u.2 ∧

                IntegrationInACVF.Section4.TranslationOffsets.offsetValue T t) ∧

                                      SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV ↑b ↑c →

@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeGammaPreparation.prepare : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
    M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
      ∀ (G : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base),
        Cardinal.mk ↑G.real < κ →
          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.VFGammaGeneratedBase G →
            ∀ {n m k : ℕ}
              (r : SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point (n + 1) m → SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 0 k → Prop),
              SpecialTransformations.Section5.GammaGraphDefinable G r →
                ∃ l B V,
                  SpecialTransformations.Section5.HeadSaturated (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) ∧
                    (∃ j, SpecialTransformations.Section5.Tagged (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) 0 j) ∧
                      ∀ (b c : ↑B.carrier),
                        SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV ↑b ↑c →
                          SpecialTransformations.Section5.TailEq ↑b ↑c →
                            ∀ (z : SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 0 k),
                              r (↑(V.inverseMap.toFun b)) z ↔ r (↑(V.inverseMap.toFun c)) z
@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeObstaclePreparation.zero_obstacles : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {S : Set M} {n m l k : ℕ} {A : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S (n + 1) m}
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S (n + 1) l} {Γ : Type} [inst : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ]
  (v : Valuation SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF Γ),
  (∀ (x y : SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF), SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ v x ≤ v y) →
    ∀ (U : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point (n + 1) k))
      (R : SpecialTransformations.Section5.VerticalRootFamily A),
      (∀ (p : SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point n m) (a : SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF)
          (r : SpecialTransformations.Section3.Balls.OpenRadius Γ),
          (∀ x ∈ SpecialTransformations.Section3.Balls.openDisc v a r,
              SpecialTransformations.Section5.insertHead x p ∈ SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier A) →
            (∀ x ∈ R.fibre p, x ∉ SpecialTransformations.Section3.Balls.openDisc v a r) →
              ∀ x ∈ SpecialTransformations.Section3.Balls.openDisc v a r,
                ∀ y ∈ SpecialTransformations.Section3.Balls.openDisc v a r,
                  ∀ (ys : Fin k → SpecialTransformations.Section5.RV),
                    ((SpecialTransformations.Section5.insertHead x p).1, ys) ∈ U ↔
                      ((SpecialTransformations.Section5.insertHead y p).1, ys) ∈ U) →
        ∀ (T : IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeBijection A B),
          SpecialTransformations.Section5.HeadSaturated (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) →
            (∀ (a : ↑A.carrier),
                ↑a ∈ SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier R.set →
                  SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑(T.toMap.toFun a)) 0 = 0) →
              ∀ (b c : ↑B.carrier),
                SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV ↑b ↑c →
                  SpecialTransformations.Section5.TailEq ↑b ↑c →
                    ∀ (ys : Fin k → SpecialTransformations.Section5.RV),
                      ((↑(T.inverseMap.toFun b)).1, ys) ∈ U ↔ ((↑(T.inverseMap.toFun c)).1, ys) ∈ U

    Cardinal.mk ↑S < κ →
      M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →

          SpecialTransformations.Section5.IsRVPullback (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier A) →

              ∀
                (f :
                  SpecialTransformations.Section5.Map A
                    (SpecialTransformations.Section5.PresentedSet.univ (SpecialTransformations.Section5.pointSpace 0 k)
                      S)),
                ∃ l B T,
                  SpecialTransformations.Section5.HeadSaturated (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) ∧
                    (∃ j, SpecialTransformations.Section5.Tagged (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) 0 j) ∧
                      ∀ (b c : ↑B.carrier),
                        SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV ↑b ↑c →
                          SpecialTransformations.Section5.TailEq ↑b ↑c →

@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeRootReduction.step_fibre_subset : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]

  (R : SpecialTransformations.Section5.VerticalRootFamily A) (h00 : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00)
  (N : ℕ) (hN : ∀ (p : SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point n m), (R.fibre p).card ≤ N) (k : Fin m)
  (hTag : SpecialTransformations.Section5.Tagged (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier A) 0 k)
  (q : SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point n (n + 1 + m)) (x : SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF),
  SpecialTransformations.Section5.insertHead x q ∈
      SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier
        (R.imageSet (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalMeanStep.step R h00 N hN k hTag).toMap) →
    x ∈
      Finset.image
        (fun y =>
          y -
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.Barycentre.mean
              (R.fibre (SpecialTransformations.Section5.VerticalRootFamily.parentBase q)))
        ({y ∈ R.fibre (SpecialTransformations.Section5.VerticalRootFamily.parentBase q) |
          SpecialTransformations.Section5.rv
              (y -
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.Barycentre.mean
                  (R.fibre (SpecialTransformations.Section5.VerticalRootFamily.parentBase q))) =
            q.2 (Fin.castAdd m 0)})

@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeBijection.inverse_pair : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {S : Set M} {n m l : ℕ} {A : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S (n + 1) m}
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S (n + 1) l} (T : IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeBijection A B)
  (a b : ↑B.carrier),
  SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV ↑a ↑b →
    SpecialTransformations.Section5.TailEq ↑a ↑b →
      SpecialTransformations.Section5.TailEq ↑(T.inverseMap.toFun a) ↑(T.inverseMap.toFun b) ∧
        SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑(T.inverseMap.toFun a)) 0 -
            SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑(T.inverseMap.toFun b)) 0 =
          SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑a) 0 - SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑b) 0
@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeBijection.inverse_slice : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {S : Set M} {n m l : ℕ} {A : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S (n + 1) m}
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S (n + 1) l} {Γ : Type} [inst : LinearOrderedCommGroupWithZero Γ]
  (v : Valuation SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF Γ),
  (∀ (x y : SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF), SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.VFValued.predV x y ↔ v x ≤ v y) →
    ∀ (T : IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeBijection A B),
      SpecialTransformations.Section5.HeadSaturated (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) →
        ∀ (b : ↑B.carrier),
          SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑b) 0 ≠ 0 →
            T.inverseSlice b =
                {p |
                  v
                        (SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt p 0 -
                          SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑(T.inverseMap.toFun b)) 0) <
                      v (SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑b) 0) ∧
                    SpecialTransformations.Section5.TailEq p ↑(T.inverseMap.toFun b) ∧
                      p.2 = (↑(T.inverseMap.toFun b)).2} ∧
              T.inverseSlice b ⊆ {p | SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV p ↑(T.inverseMap.toFun b)}

  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]

  (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap.fromSpecial T).toFun a =
      (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap.fromSpecial T).toFun b ↔
    SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV
      ↑(T.special.toMap.toFun (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap.specialSource T a))

@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.TranslationOffsets.inverse_fibre_translate : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {S : Set M} {m l : ℕ} {A : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S 1 m}
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S 1 l} (T : SpecialTransformations.Section5.SpecialBijection A B),
  SpecialTransformations.Section5.IsRVPullback (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) →
    ∀ (b : ↑B.carrier),
      (fun p => SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt p 0) '' T.inverseFibre b =
        (fun x =>
            x +
              IntegrationInACVF.Section4.TranslationOffsets.offsetValue T
                (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.TranslationOffsets.toLabels B b)) ''
          {x |

  SpecialTransformations.Section5.GammaSetDefinable G C →
    IntegrationInACVF.Section4.FiniteScalarFamilies.Graph (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaBaseBridge.fullBase G)
      (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.family
        (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPositionFamilies.domain B C))
      (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.value
        (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPositionFamilies.position T C))
@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPositionFamilies.head_body : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {S : Set M} {l : ℕ} {B : SpecialTransformations.Section5.DefSet S 2 l}
  (T :
    IntegrationInACVF.Section4.RelativeBijection
      (SpecialTransformations.Section5.PresentedSet.univ (SpecialTransformations.Section5.pointSpace 2 0) S) B)
  (j : Fin l),
  SpecialTransformations.Section5.Tagged (SpecialTransformations.Section5.carrier B) 0 j →
    ∀ (b : ↑B.carrier),
      IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalOffsets.headBody T b =
        {y |
          ∃ a,
            SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑a) 0 = y ∧
              SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑a) 1 = SpecialTransformations.Section5.vfAt (↑b) 1 ∧
                (↑(T.toMap.toFun a)).2 = (↑b).2}
@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPresentation.exists_presentation : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
    M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
      ∀ (G : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base),
        Cardinal.mk ↑G.real < κ →
          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.VFGammaGeneratedBase G →
            ∀ (A : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 2 0)),
              SpecialTransformations.Section5.GammaSetDefinable G A →
                Nonempty (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPresentation G A)

@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap.pairFibre : {M : Type} →
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] →
    [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] →
      {G : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} →
        {d : ℕ} →
          {A : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 2 0)} →
            IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap G A d →
              ↑A → Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF × SpecialTransformations.Section5.VF)
@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap.pairFibre_polydisc : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {G : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {d : ℕ}
  {A : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 2 0)} (P : IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap G A d),
  (∀ (a b : ↑A), P.toFun a = P.toFun b → SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV ↑a ↑b) →
    ∀ (a₀ : ↑A),
      ∃ u v,
        ∀ p ∈ P.pairFibre a₀, SpecialTransformations.Section5.rv p.1 = u ∧ SpecialTransformations.Section5.rv p.2 = v

              ↑(IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaFamilySyntax.domain
                  (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.family V.domain))

@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPresentation.pullback : {M : Type} →
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] →
    [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] →
      {G : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} →
        {A : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 2 0)} →
          {d : ℕ} →
            (V : IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPresentation G A) →
              IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap G
                  (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaFamilySyntax.domain
                    (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.family V.domain))
                  d →
                IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap G A d
@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPresentation.pullback_fibre : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {G : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {A : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 2 0)}
  {d : ℕ} (V : IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPresentation G A)
  (Q :
    IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap G
      (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaFamilySyntax.domain
        (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.family V.domain))
      d)
  (a₀ : ↑A)
  (row :
    IntegrationInACVF.Section4.PositionRow Q (V.scalarProjection a₀)
      (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.value V.position) V.paradigm_index),
  (V.pullback Q).pairFibre a₀ =
    IntegrationInACVF.Section4.CellsFromFibres.relation row.domain row.position
      ((↑(V.scalarProjection a₀)).2 V.paradigm_index)
@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPresentation.pullback_cells : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {G : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {A : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 2 0)}
  {d : ℕ} (V : IntegrationInACVF.Section4.VerticalPresentation G A)
  (Q :
    IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaRVMap G
      (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaFamilySyntax.domain
        (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.family V.domain))
      d),
  (∀
      (a b :
        ↑(IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaFamilySyntax.domain
            (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.family V.domain))),
      Q.toFun a = Q.toFun b → SpecialTransformations.Section5.SameRV ↑a ↑b) →
    (∀
        (a :
          ↑(IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaFamilySyntax.domain
              (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.family V.domain))),
        Nonempty
          (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.PositionRow Q a (IntegrationInACVF.Section4.GammaScalarFamilies.value V.position)
            V.paradigm_index)) →
      ∀ (a₀ : ↑A), IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.IsTwoCell ((V.pullback Q).pairFibre a₀)
@IntegrationInACVF.Section4.geometric_decomposition : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
  Cardinal.aleph0 ≤ κ →
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
      M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
        ∀ (G : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base),
          Cardinal.mk ↑G.real < κ →
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.VFGammaGeneratedBase G →
              ∀ δ ∈ G.gamma,
                1 < δ →
                  ∀ (A : Set (SpecialTransformations.Section5.Point 2 0)),
                    SpecialTransformations.Section5.GammaSetDefinable G A →
                      ∃ d P, ∀ (a₀ : ↑A), IntegrationInACVF.Section4.Cells.IsTwoCell (P.pairFibre a₀)
